Hosts draw their own per-node layers in `paintNodeOverlay` (called once per pass; `nodeLifted`) and gestures in `paintOverlay` (last). The circuit editor's node move is live the same way (on the 4-unit grid). A face on a node scaled below `kFaceMiniatureScale` (0.25) has no controls (a press there is the block's). A cable's tube is as wide as the smaller of its ends' scales allows (≤ 1) and each plug follows its own block's scale (0.1 … 2): a tiny block's plugs are tiny.
- **Orbiting main ports, floating face panels, Delete on a cable** (TX.orbit-ports, SPEC §15 2026-11-03 "orbiting ports, a round VCA, Delete on a cable"; `pq/views/circuit/orbit.h`, `src/circuit/orbit.cpp`). A closed device block's MAIN input (`Node::mainIn`, from `doc::mainPortsOf`; not an anchored control) and main output (output 0) orbit its OUTLINE (`circuit::Outline`: a circle, or a rect with rounded corners): every face reports its shape (`Face::shape()` → `FaceShape`: round with centre and radius, or its rect's corner radius; `faceShape(type)` cached per type; `PanelFace::kPanelCorner`; a plain block's body inside its 8-unit pin margins), and nothing is special-cased per type. One rule, shared by the layout and the canvas (`solveOrbits`): each port points from the outline's centre at its cables' other ends (an end that orbits too: its block's centre; an anchored control: its centre; any other pin: the pin), several cables their mean direction, none (or cancelling) the default (in: left, out: right); it sits at the outline point on that ray pushed out along the normal by `kOrbitPortR + kOrbitGap` (× the block's scale); a pinned port keeps its angle; an unpinned port steps along the outline (±2°, ≤ 90 steps) until it clears the block's other pins and the other port by `kOrbitSpacing`; pinned ports place first. **Model** (`project_patch::Builder::orbit`, after `layout`, before `route`): `Node::outline`, `orbitIn` / `orbitOut` (`on`, `pinned`, `angle`, `at`); `outPortPoint` / `inPortPoint` are where an output / input meets its wires (the routes, the cables' rest shapes, `placeAt`'s spawn point). Pins are view state: `doc::PatchNudge::inPinned / outPinned / inAngle / outAngle` (radians, quantised to what a file keeps: `patchPinQuantise`; saved as the nudge entry's optional `pinIn` / `pinOut` in degrees), not undoable, like a nudge. **Scene**: `SceneNode::outline`, `ScenePin::orbit / pinned / angle / orbitKey` (the scene pin at the settled place). **Canvas**: per orbiting pin an `OrbitRef` (its angle now `cur`, its `target`); a new scene keeps each port's angle by key and eases it (`kOrbitEaseSeconds`, snap within `kOrbitSnap`) to the scene's; `setNodeDrag` solves again only the nodes in `orbitLive` (the lifted blocks and those with a cable to one; built when the lift set changes) at the drag's offset, so the ports turn live during a lifted drag; frames only while a port slides (`portsMoving`); `setCablesAnimated(false)` lands them at once. Cables end on the port where it is now (`cableEnds`), hit tests use it (`pinPoint`; the grid query widened by a port's reach), the face does not draw it (its `FaceJack::visible` is false). `paintPorts` after the cables in each pass: an input a hollow ring, an output a filled disc with a bright core and an additive glow, in its first cable's colour (idle: `face.jackRing`), the block's scale; the hover ring. `setPortAngle(pin, angle)` / `releasePort(pin)`: a host holds a port where its drag puts it (the other port gives way). **Gestures** (`ProjectCanvas`): an input port pressed and dragged slides along the edge (`Drag::Port`); an output port starts its cable as before but becomes a slide while the pointer stays within `kPortBandPx` of its orbit over `kPortSlidePx`, and a slide pulled `kPortLeavePx` off the edge becomes the cable again; the release pins it (`ProjectPatchView::pinPort`), a double-click unpins (`unpinPort`). Inside a block's outline a press hits a port only on its disc (far out a port is a dot on the block's edge: the block stays grabbable); `wireRefusal` / `finishWire` take an input pin's block for a drop on a port outside it. **Floating face panels**: `Face::popupRect()` (face-local, may reach outside `worldSize`; nullopt: closed), `paintPopup`, `setPopupOverFace` (a host without room around the face — the Device view's `FaceDisplay` — opens it over the face): the canvas hit-tests it as part of its face before anything else (`popupUnder`; over the cables too), paints it last (`paintPopups`, after `paintOverlay`), and a press elsewhere blurs the face (`focusLost`: it closes; repainted). The VCA's range and curve box live there (nothing in the layout moves). **Delete on a cable**: `ProjectCanvas::onKey` deletes the hovered cable's document wire (`ProjectPatchView::deleteCable`: one undo step) before the selection; the base canvas's click state ends on every release the project canvas handles, so the hover follows the pointer after a click. Scenes `project-orbit-ports`, `project-vca-panel` (`--project tests/views/fixtures/meta-cables`).
- **The mixer as a face, at the band's top right** (TX.mixer-look, bug report 2026-11-03; `src/project_patch/mixer_face.cpp`, `internal.h`). A mixer node is drawn as `MixerFace` (a `Face` hosted by the canvas like a device panel: the scene adds a `SceneFace` with `panel` set, the track as `owner` and `mixerFacePath` — `kMixerFacePrefix`, its size and its send pins — as `path`; `ProjectPatchView::makePanel` makes it for that prefix; `syncPanels` / `panelOf` touch device nodes only). Four static layers in tiles keyed by its geometry (body, tracks, ticks, frame); the live layer reads the track from the document at each paint (a mixer edit repaints, never rebuilds): header (track colour, MIXER / MASTER, M = the activator, S), pan knob (±135°), volume fader on Live's law (`volumeToFader`) with ticks +6 … −48 dB, a stereo level meter on the fader's scale (the view's `MeterSource`, polled through the faces' voice-reader clock — a `VoiceTapReader` adapter as the Spectrum / Scope faces do; a poll repaints only when a drawn end moves a quarter unit), a row per send (`metrics::kMixerSendTop`, the send pin level with it: `pinOffset` for `NodeKind::Mixer`). Its controls are the face's (one store gesture per drag, double-click resets); a press on its frame is the canvas's (`Drag::MaybeMoveMixer` / `MoveMixer`: lifted like a block, dropped in its own band → `ProjectPatchView::placeMixer`). **Placement**: `placeChain` puts the mixer right of the band's main path AND its off-path row, at the band's top (`y + kTitle`; the master's Out level with its output); after the nudges `Builder::placeMixer` keeps it there — pushed right of any block nudged past it — or, when placed (`doc::PatchNudge::mixerPlaced`, `mixerX` / `mixerY` from the band's top-left, on the track's entry; saved as optional `mixerX` / `mixerY`, both or neither), at that offset (never above the title strip); a band grows to hold its mixer. View state like a nudge (not undoable); Reset Layout and the mixer menu's Mixer to Top Right (`unplaceMixer`) clear it. Scene `project-mixer`.
- **Pan laws** (TX.pan-laws, `model.h` PanMode / panMatrix, `lower.h` "Pan law", schema 18): `Mixer::panMode` (Balance default; Constant Power, Linear −6 dB, True Stereo) is structural: `SetTrackPanMode` recompiles that track only (its exports do not change). The law is block-rate math on the pan param folded into each gain stage's one smoother; True Stereo adds one smoothed 2×2 matrix (gain + gain_add on the swapped channels). The mixer face shows the law as a tag above the knob (a click or the mixer's menu → Pan Law).
- **Circuit editor** (T4.4, `pq/views/circuit/patch_editor.h`, `circuit_editor.h`, `patch_player.h`; `src/circuit/patch_editor.cpp`, `circuit_editor.cpp`): `PatchEditor` is the document — a .pqp file's text as the one source of truth, re-parsed after every edit with the previous parse (`ParseOptions::previous`, lenient layout, deferred missing inputs), so NodeIds and state keys survive edits from either pane (stdlib sweep: 100 % of state entries kept). Text-pane edits are `replaceText`; circuit edits run a `graph::edit` operation on the drawn patch (stored layout + positions carried by NodeId + completed) and then either splice the layout (`spliceLayout`: move, tidy, save keep hand formatting) or reprint the edited patch's `patch … }` span (structural edits; the re-parse takes ids from the edited IR); a text that does not lower keeps the last good parse (the canvas veils it) and refuses circuit edits. One undo history of text replacements (typing coalesces per run) for both panes. `check()` = infer → optimise → schedule of the shown / playing patch at the player's rate, errors as inline diagnostics; a clean schedule of the playing patch goes to the `PatchPlayer` (plain data: `sampleRate`, `play(schedule, scheduled)` = start or hot swap, `stop`, `stateLayout` = ScheduleOptions::previous). **Seam for T4.1 / T4.13**: `PatchEditor::onCommitted(text, parse)` fires after every edit whose text lowered — a device patch edited in place hands that text to its document command there, and the project compile (ProjectLink, per-track units) swaps the unit instead of a PatchPlayer. `CircuitEditor` (a full-window `ui::Popover`): title bar (Back, file › patch path, Play, Tidy, Fit, Save, Close), a `Splitter` with `EditCanvas` (a `CircuitCanvas` with editing gestures: drag pins to connect — hidden optional pins show under the pointer —, drag nodes to move, double-click to add / rename, keys in docs/HOTKEYS.md) and a `ui::CodeEditor` text pane (`highlightPatchLine` colours, marks from the diagnostics), a status line; the check runs 50 ms after the last edit (one timer, then idle). View > Open Patch in Circuit View… (`view.openPatch`) opens files in it; `setPatchPlayer(shell, player)` gives it the app's preview. **Probes and cost (T4.5, `pq/views/circuit/probes.h`)**: while the shown patch plays (it, or an instance inside it: the editor keeps the call path of the subcircuits it opened), every bool net gets a level-only probe and its wires are drawn lit (`setWireLit`, polled at 30 Hz) in any block it was high; resting on a wire 150 ms compiles a probe on its source (`PatchEditor::setProbes` → the next check; the player swaps in the bit-identical program) and shows a readout at the pointer (`ProbeOverlay`: the source's name, the value or HIGH / LOW, a mini-scope of the last 1024 frames from the probe's ring; PQ Meter's signal look, theme `circuit.probe*`); the probe stays compiled 1.5 s after the pointer leaves. The title bar's Cost toggle turns the player's meter on (`PatchPlayer::setMetering`) and labels nodes (a subcircuit call with its instance) and wires (inserted voice → global sums, global → voice copies) with ns per sample (`attributeCost` over `PatchEditor::played()`, the played schedule kept in NodeIds), 4 Hz, smoothed, overlapping labels left out costliest first; the status line has the total and its share of a core. `PatchPlayer` (plain data) gained `setProbeSignals`, `probeSignal(id)`, `probeLevel(id)`, `setMetering`, `readCost` → `CostReading { generation, totalNsPerFrame, regionNsPerFrame, units }`, `generation()`. Scenes `circuit-editor-probe` (stdlib/envelopes/adsr.pqp), `circuit-editor-cost` (stdlib/filters/svf.pqp) run on a scene player with deterministic data. `CircuitCanvas::setScene(…, keepTiles)` keeps symbol body tiles across an edit's scene rebuild. Scenes `circuit-editor`, `circuit-editor-error` (patch scenes). Colours: theme group `code.*`. **Legibility (T4.15, `pq/views/circuit/node_info.h`, `pq/graph/structure.h`)**: `graph::analyseStructure(patch, args)` evaluates the patch's compile-time constants as written (literals, const-rate `in`s with their `ConstArg` arguments or defaults, pure kernel atoms through the optimiser's own fold, `src/graph/src/fold.h`, shared with `optimise.cpp`; params never) and marks the arms of select / mux that a constant selector does not pick DEAD; a node is inactive when it reaches no sink except through dead wires (exactly what the optimiser drops); `callArguments` carries constants into a call's callee. The editor analyses the shown patch with the instance's arguments (`CircuitEditor::setInstance`: a device's settings and params, kept current by the device host) carried down its call stack, and passes the masks to `buildScene` (`SceneOptions::inactiveNodes / inactiveWires / hideInactive / relayoutVisible`; `SceneNode/SceneWire::inactive, hidden`: hidden parts keep their index but have no grid cells, segments, labels or bounds; `layoutSubset` lays the visible nodes out on their own when the text stores none of their positions). The title bar's Inactive toggle draws them dimmed instead (`ViewContext::viewPrefs()`, saved by the app in its settings "views" section); while hidden, `PatchEditor::setLayoutBase` makes circuit edits start from the shown layout and `PatchEditor::tidy(patch, only)` re-places only the visible nodes. Node tooltips: the editor runs its own rest timer (`OverlayLayer::tooltipDelay()`) and shows `nodeTooltip()` text through `OverlayLayer::showTooltip` (multi-line now), built once per hover; constants show `recognisedForm()` (p/q, π forms, roots, logs, whole dB; ≤ 1 float ulp). Scenes `circuit-inactive-hidden`, `circuit-inactive-shown`, `circuit-tooltip` (stdlib/devices/saturator.pqp). TMM.12: the one circuit applies the same hiding — `circuit::buildDeviceScene(callee, settings)` (`pq/views/circuit/chain_circuit.h`) analyses a device's patch with its settings and hides what they switch off (relaid out unless the patch's stored layout places the visible nodes); the project patch view's opened devices and opened bands (`ChainSceneCache`, keyed by `deviceSceneKey`: patch + settings) use it, and the composed scenes carry hidden wires (no points) and the devices' oversample washes. So Operator opened in place shows its 466 visible of 720 nodes (GU.4: the Meta Operator, the one patch with a stored layout, is deleted).
- **Device chains** (T4.1, `pq/document/model.h` Device, `commands.h`, `edit_ops.h`, `devices.h`, `lower.h`; `pq/views/device_chain/device_chain.h`; QUESTIONS Q129). `Track::devices` = the ordered chain of `Device { DeviceId id (the one IdSource); patch; kind Instrument | AudioEffect; active; params, settings: sparse sorted (name, value) — only what the user set, values normalised }`; chain rule: one instrument, first, on MIDI tracks only. Commands `AddDevice`, `RemoveDevice`, `MoveDevice` (within / across tracks), `SetDeviceActive`, `SetDeviceSetting` (Structural), `SetDeviceParam` (Param: `ParamRef{DeviceParam, device, fnv1a32(name)}`, automatable since T4.18: see "Device automation"); `ChangeSet::devices`; builders `ops::addDevice` (an instrument replaces the track's), `deleteDevices`, `moveDevice`, `setDevicesActive` / `toggleDevicesActive`, `pasteDevices`, `duplicateDevices`, `setDeviceParam`, `setDeviceSetting`; `ops::createTrack` gives a MIDI track the default instrument (`kDefaultInstrument` BasicSynth), `makeTrack` an empty chain. **Device library** (`devices.h`): `loadDeviceLibrary(stdlibDir)` parses `stockDevices()` (BasicSynth, Utility, Saturator, EQ Eight; a missing file is left out) with their imports into one `PatchLibrary` + a `DeviceCatalog` (`describeDevice`: kind, params with range / unit / label / curve / steps, settings with literal defaults) + `pathOf(patch)`; `app::loadDeviceLibrary`, `ProjectLink::devices()`, `ViewContext::setDeviceLibrary` (the window gives the link's; `renderViewScene(…, devices)`). **Lowering**: per lowered device (active, in the library, of its kind) a subcircuit call `d<id>` with NodeId `nodeIdFor(Device, id, 0)` (state keys follow the device: reorder / add / remove keep every other device's state); the chain is wired left → right (k = 1: `pack(x, x)` when a mono signal meets a stereo input); MIDI = tuning nodes → instrument → `voice_sum` → effects → stages (no active instrument: x = 0, role Audio, no event slot); group / return / master: input → effects → stages; params unconnected → runtime params `d<id>__<name>`, `LoweredParam` key `dev<id>:<name>` (`lowerParams(p, tuningNodes, library)`); settings → literal constants (ids from a per-track pool 256 … 1023); a missing device is bypassed (`LoweredProject::missingDevices`, the status line). **Link**: device-param-only ChangeSets write just those params (`applyDeviceParams`, O(changed)); device param slots live while their device does; `jit_cache.cpp`'s stock projects add a MIDI track with each stock effect (Q116). **Device tab** (`DeviceChainView`, `installDeviceChain`, `findDeviceChain`; `src/device_chain/`): the selected track's (else the detail clip's track's) chain, one T4.6 `devices::DevicePanel` per device (description `<stem>.panel.json` next to the device file, else `autoPanel`) whose `DeviceTarget` is the document's device (param gestures = one store gesture; consts → `SetDeviceSetting`; activator → `SetDeviceActive`; external changes back through `setParamValue` / `setConstValue` / `setActive`); add slot + device menu, title-bar drag to reorder, right-click menu, `device.*` view actions (scope `devices`). T4.6's stand-alone preview (`devices::DeviceView`) shares the slot and covers it while open. Scene `device-chain`.
- **Device automation** (T4.18, M4 review H4; `model.h`, `lower.h`, `serialize.h` schema 9, `app/project_link.cpp`, `views/arrangement/automation.h`, `views/device_chain/`). **Document**: `isAutomatable(ParamRef)` takes device params (a rack's macros and selector included), wire amounts and a chain's volume / pan (`ParamRef::chain(c, kChainVolume / kChainPan)`; `isDeviceParamKind`). A DeviceParam lane carries the param's name (`AutomationLane::paramName`; its hash is `target`). Such a lane lives on `laneHome(p, ref)`, the track holding the device / the chain's rack / the wire's target (`checkLaneParam`); `apply` ends every Structural command with `finishDeviceLanes`, which moves lanes whose device changed track and removes those whose device, chain or wire is gone — in the same command, so it is one undo step, and after the whole Batch, so Group / Ungroup / Hot-Swap keep the lanes of devices that keep their ids (`replaceDevice` drops lanes of params the new patch lacks). The document leaves a device param's value range open (the patch's range applies: the link clamps, the views edit in the knob's range). **Lowering / link**: lanes drive the existing slots at block rate (`AutomationLaneDesc::perSample = false`), keyed `paramKey(lane)`: `dev<id>:<name>`, `wire<id>:amount`, `chain<id>:volume` / `:pan`. A chain with a volume or pan lane lowers `c<id>_volume` (dB) / `c<id>_pan` and its gain law in the graph (nodes keyed by the chain's lowest lane id, `chainMixerLane`) instead of the derived `gl` / `gr`; the chain's `gain` stage smooths either. A macro-mapped param plays its macro (no runtime param: its lane is inert, and the views do not offer it). **Views**: the lane chooser has a section per device (in chain order: a rack's visible macros and selector, then its chains' volume / pan, then the devices inside) and one for the wires into the track's devices; device lanes are drawn in the knob's scale (`ParamDisplay::Scale::Log` / `Db` / linear) and named "<device>: <param>". `ViewContext::showAutomation` (`view_change::kShowAutomation`): a knob's (and a chain row's) "Show Automation" points the device's track's follow lane at it (TL.5; a pinned parameter: its lane revealed). The Device view marks automated controls with an LED (grey: overridden by hand, or macro-mapped) and moves their knobs to the envelope at the playhead (`DeviceChainView::refreshAutomation` / `applyAutomation`; a 30 Hz timer only while playing). TL.6: the mechanism is shared with the project patch (band) view — `pq/views/arrangement/automation_follow.h` `AutomationFollow` (collect the lanes with points of the shown tracks with their overridden / macro-mapped state, `valueAt` the envelope at the playhead, the 30 Hz poll); each view shows the values on its controls: `PanelTarget::showPosition` (a document panel target forwards it to its native engines' faces), `MetaTarget::showPosition` (`DocTarget::value` reads a shown position through the param's curve), the band view's mixer face (`mixerShow`: volume / pan / sends). Scene `device-automation`.
- **Device panels** (T4.6; GU.11: a LEAF block's Device-view frame — `pq/views/devices/device_model.h`, `panel_layout.h`, `device_panel.h`, `analysis.h`; `src/devices/`): `signatureOf(patch)` → `DeviceSignature` (params with label, unit, range, curve; structural consts; exports); `autoPanel(sig)` (one knob / box / toggle per param and setting) or `facePanel(sig, …)` (a native engine's face, scaled to the frame's height) → `PanelLayout`; `DevicePanel` = jack strip, title bar (activator, fold, title, preset slot, rename), body; it reports edits to a `DeviceTarget` (param gestures, settings, activation, the engine's meta target / voices / output spectrum) and repaints only what an external change touches. `SpectrumFeed` (analysis.h) is the analysis worker over a `TapRing` the Spectrum / Oscilloscope / Spectrogram faces read. (The `.panel.json` descriptions, their displays and the stand-alone device preview are in retired/.)
- **Cross-device wiring** (T4.7, `pq/document/model.h` Wire, `commands.h`, `edit_ops.h`, `lower.h`; `pq/views/device_chain/wiring.h`; `src/device_chain/wiring.cpp`; QUESTIONS Q135). **Document**: `Project::wires` (sorted by id; schema 4) of `Wire {id, from device + export, to device + param, amount [−1, 1], reducer}`; AddWire / RemoveWire / SetWireReducer Structural, SetWireAmount Param (`ParamRef::wireAmount`); RemoveDevice / RemoveTrack drop the removed devices' wires in the same command (TMM.10-fix2: RemoveTrack also the wires from its note source); TMM.10-fix2: a wire's source may be a MIDI track's note source (`Wire::noteSource`, `from` invalid, `port` ∈ `kNoteOutputs` hz / gate / velocity; `ops::connectNoteWire`; schema 12), lowered as that track's voice terminals (tuned hz, gate_to_real(gate), velocity): per voice into a device of its track that runs per voice, through the wire's reducer into any other (across tracks as `export m<wire>`), Inactive while the track plays no voices (lower.h "Modulation wires"); builders `ops::connectWire`, `deleteWires`, `setWireAmount`, `setWireReducer`, `wiresInto` / `wiresFrom`. **Lowering** (`lower.h` "Modulation wires"): `planWires` reads delay-free port dependencies from the device patches (z1 / delay lines split a path, phasor's hz does not: the scheduler's rule), adds the chains, routing and non-delayed sends, and takes wires in id order: a wire whose target reaches its source is Delayed (a `z1` on it), a cross-track wire closing a cycle through two instrument tracks is Refused (`WireState::reason` TwoInstruments), and so is (TMM.13-fix1 H3, VoiceLoop) a delayed wire whose loop passes through both per-voice and global signal points (a voice reducer inside the loop: its z1 would need the voice groups and the global part to alternate per sample, which the scheduler refuses — it silenced the whole unit), a wire with a switched-off end Inactive, with an unknown port / bool param Invalid (`doc::wireStates`, `LoweredProject::wires`). A modulated param becomes the root param `d<id>__<name>` → position (param rate) → + Σ `w<wire>`·source → clip → travel map (T4.7's `doc::paramTravel`; GU.1: the `curve` atom, see **Curves**) → the device pin; across tracks `export m<wire>` (first channel, voice-reduced) → ProjectWire Modulation → `in m<wire>`. Node ids: `NodeRole::Wire` (48 slots per wire id). **Taps**: per modulated param `export tap_d<id>_<param>` = (swept up, swept down) ≥ 0, `ProjectGraph::taps` (buses after the output's, metered like track outs); ProjectLink implements `views::ModulationSource` (`readModulation`) and reads every metered bus through one path (`readLevels`) for both the meters and the rings; `LinkStats::delayedWires` / `refusedWires`, the status line names a new one; `wireStates()`. **Views**: `CableLayer` in `ShellSlot::Cables` (the root's child between main and overlay, not hittable; badges are its hittable children; hittable itself only while a cable is dragged, after taking the pointer from the jack strip on the first move, so the drag survives the Device view switching tracks). Ends from `DeviceChainView::jackPoint` / `paramPoint` / `paramControls` (the T4.6 hooks `jacks()`, `controlFor`, new `onJackDrag`), else the track's row in the arrangement; recomputed in `TreeObserver::onLayout` (ui: called after every layout pass) with only moved cables damaged. Rings: `DeviceChainView::refreshRings` (static range knob ± Σ|amount|) and `pollRings` (30 Hz timer only while `ModulationSource::modulationLive()` and a modulated knob is shown); `ui::ModulationRing` gained `liveLo` / `liveHi`. Actions `view.cycleCables` (View menu) and `wire.delete` (`ViewContext::kWiringActions`); `ViewContext::setModulationSource` (kModulation). Scene `device-wiring`. Stock device `lfo_device` (LFO) as a modulation source. **TX.patching** (SPEC §15 2026-11-01 "a wire adds its value in the target param's own units", "free patching in the circuit"; QUESTIONS Q?-TX.patching-a): `Wire::law` — **Add** for every new wire (`ops::connectWire` / `connectNoteWire`: no amount, `amount` stays 1; SetWireAmount and amount lanes refused), **Travel** only for wires migrated from schema ≤ 12 (`ops::connectTravelWire` / `connectTravelNoteWire` rebuild such a project; never offered by the views). A wire's source is any output of its device (`port` an `out` or an export; TX.bool-real: a bool `out` too, lowered as its 0 / 1 — `gate_to_real` at the source, before the wire's reducer), its target any real param or non-const real `in` (`param` names either). Lowering (`lower.h` "Modulation wires", TX.patching paragraph): params `clip(centre + Σ src, lo, hi)` in the param's normalised unit (no clip for `@wrap`; TMM.18: `@signed` clips to ±max(|lo|, |hi|) instead — `meta::ParamSpec::signedValue`, GU.4: the Phase Accumulator module's Frequency value block has a signed curve, so a negative frequency runs its phase backwards; rounded for `@steps`), taps in units (`ModulationTap::units`); inputs read the chain's signal (or nothing) + Σ src (same track: every channel); (GU.4: `meta::ParamSpec::unitsInBody` / `noKnobCurve` / `Builder::units` are gone with the VCA: no meta type applies a law of its own in its body, every knob's law is its `dsp::Curve`); a chain meta device's wired inputs become `in`s of its expansion (`meta::ExpandContext::wired`). Migrated Travel wires keep T4.7's ends and math exactly. **Free devices** (TX.patching; since TX.all-free simply the blocks off their track's main path, see **Main path** below: `Device::free`, `SetDeviceFree`, `ops::setDevicesFree`, "Take Out of Chain / Put Into Chain" and the "◌ free" title bar are gone): called after the path, never chained; a meta block off the path plays its Nested form in the track's voice context when the track plays voices (so the compiler decides what runs per voice). Views: the Device view's rings convert unit taps to knob travel (no static span for an added wire), an added wire's badge is a plus (no amount drag; Pointer cursor). **Curves** (GU.1, SPEC §15 2026-11-06 "built GUIs"; docs/plans/GU.md "Curves"; QUESTIONS Q?-GU.1-a; replaces TX.knob-curves' `doc::KnobCurve` / `ParamCurve` / `meta::Curve` and their per-curve atom chains). ONE curve model and evaluator, `pq/dsp/curve.h`: `dsp::Curve` = min, max, `CurveUnit`, step, ≤ 16 segments (points (t, y) with y the share of [min, max]; per segment a `CurveShape` Linear / Exp / Log / Power / SCurve / Joined (GU.4: a + e·x′ + b·expm1(c·x′) from the segment's flat end, slope-matched to its neighbour: the VCA's C1-joined log-spaced outer) and a bend), `CurveMode` Clip / Wrap / Signed, `CurveOutput` None / DbToGain (a dB curve whose value is a linear gain); `curvePreset` (Linear, Octave — alias `exp` —, Exponential / Logarithmic with K = ±4.8, Exp0 with its k; GU.4: `Vca` with its fine zone — negative outer, linear fine zone through 0, positive outer, the TMM.14 law exactly in double for every range and zone), `evalCurve` (double: the UI, the lowering, the migration), `curvePosition` (the inverse: analytic per segment, the reaching position nearest the hint, the hint itself when it already gives the value; unreachable values snap to an extreme), `curveClass` (the structural part: segment count, shapes, flags, step) and `compileCurve` → `CurveData` (576 bytes, float coefficients on x = p − t0; `sameData` / `curveHash`). The `curve(x, convert, slot)` ATOM (`Atom::Curve`, `Resource::Curve`, `kernels_curve.cpp`) reads a `CurveSlot` (`std::atomic<const CurveData*>`, retired through an `EpochReclaimer`): its kernel is specialised per call on the data's class (one linear segment, one Exp segment, the general multi-segment loop; SVML exact expm1 / log1p / pow / exp10; 1 lane == 8 lanes bitwise); `convert` 0 skips the dB → gain output (a Units wire adds after it); without a slot it is the identity. Interpreter and JIT call the same kernel (JIT: a kernel call), so they agree bit for bit. **Document**: every non-rack param (device params, nested meta params, lanes, presets) STORES ITS KNOB POSITION in [0, 1] (schema 20: migration 19 → 20 marks the file `valuesInUnits` and lifts its `"curves"` names to `legacyCurves`; `parseProject` then converts every value, preset, lane and nested meta param through the library's curves — `knob_positions.h` `migrateToKnobPositions` — given `LoadOptions::devices`, and refuses such a file without it; presets older than 20 holding params are refused). `Device::curves` (`ControlCurve{param, dsp::Curve}`, sorted; absent = the declared curve, `graph::declaredCurve(decl)` / `meta::paramCurve(spec)`); `SetParamCurve{device, param, optional curve, structural}`: Structural when the class changes, else a Param change; `ops::setParamCurve` / `setParamCurvePreset`, `ops::setDeviceParam` / `setMetaParam` take positions, `ops::setDeviceParamValue` / `setMetaParamValue` units (through `curvePosition`, hint = the stored position). `controlCurve`, `paramPosition`, `paramValue`, `deviceParamPosition` / `deviceParamCurve` / `deviceParamValue`, `metaParamPosition` / `metaParamCurve` / `metaParamValue` (units). Racks keep their 0 … 127 macros and selector and the old travel code. **Lowering** (`lower.h` "Modulation wires"): an UNDRIVEN control (no lowered wire, no mapping, no automation lane — an empty lane drives) is its value in units, evaluated on the UI thread: no op, also inside instrument groups. A DRIVEN control is its knob position param `d<id>__<name>@pos` (`doc::kPositionKeySuffix`, `LoweredParam::position`) → + Add wires in position (`addsInUnits`: a linear curve adds in units after it instead) / Travel wires · amount → one `curve` atom on its own slot (`LoweredProject::curves`: `CurveSite` device, param, slot, curve) → + legacy Units wires (after a dB curve: `convert` 0, then `db_to_gain`) → the pin. `LowerOptions::curveSlotOf` numbers the slots; without it the lowering numbers them and owns them (`graph::ProjectGraph::curveSlots` / `curveOwner`, adopted by `ProjectCompiler` when given no `Resources::curves`). **Link**: `ProjectLink` keeps `kCurveSlots` = 4096 slots by key, publishes a curve edit into the slot atomically (no recompile when its class stays), writes an undriven param's value or a driven one's position. **Views**: `devices::ParamInfo::curve` / `declared`, `toPosition` / `fromPosition`, `storedPosition` / `storedCurve` / `storedValue`, `knobCurves` (the presets a control offers), `curveSwitched`, `sweptPosition` (a ring's sweep in its tap's domain: `ModulationTap::Domain` Position / Curve / Dsp); `knobCurveMenu` switches presets (`setParamCurvePreset`); automation lanes of device params are positions, drawn Linear 0 … 1 and read out through the curve. No curve editor yet, and curves are neither automated nor modulated. Scene / golden `device-knob-curves`.
- **User devices; the circuit editor on the project's devices** (T4.4-fix1): retired by GU.11 — a block has no patch text of its own (`Device::source`, `.pqdevice`, `DeviceLibrary::resolve`, `editDevicePatch`, Revert to Stock, `pq/views/circuit/device_circuit.h`, `app::DevicePlayer` are gone). The circuit editor works on loose `.pqp` files; GU.f7 deleted what stayed without a user: `app::DeviceInspector` with `ProjectLink::setDeviceInspect` and the engine's `TrackInspect` / `UnitInspect` (`ProjectCompileOptions::inspect`, `CompiledUnit::inspect`), and `CircuitEditor::Host` (with its Track button, `closeSoon`).
- **Project patch view** (T4.13, SPEC §15 2026-10-24 / 2026-10-26; `pq/views/project_patch/project_patch.h`, `project_patch_view.h`, `device_cost.h`; `src/project_patch/`; QUESTIONS Q139). A view mode of the arrangement (`ShellSlot::Patch`, View > Arrangement / Project Patch, `view.togglePatch` Ctrl+Alt+P; it and the mixer view hide each other): the whole project as one editable circuit. **One model**: `buildProjectPatch(project, lowered, catalog, options)` reads the lowered `ProjectGraph` (`doc::lower` with the view's device library; the view lowers only while shown and only after a structural / meta change or a send turned on / off; PF.2: it takes the playback link's lowering instead, `ViewContext::currentLowering`, when that is of the very snapshot — shown after edits made while hidden; after an edit while shown the link's arrives a debounce later, so the view lowers itself) back onto the document — `Band` per track in lane order (`forEachTrack`; deactivated tracks keep a dim band; members of folded groups are shown), `Node`s: Lane (MIDI / audio: the band's lane, TL.1 — hidden in the scene, its rect the lane strip) or Σ (group / return / master: every Audio wire into `in`), one Device block per chain device (`lowered` = the call `d<id>` with NodeId `nodeIdFor(Device, id, 0)` is in the track patch; deactivated / missing ones dim and bypassed; pins: audio ins, the params a wire drives, out + exports), the Mixer strip (`in`, `out`, a send pin per lowered send; TL.4: its face at `metrics::kMixerScale` = 2× by default times the track's `PatchNudge::scale`, S-drag resizes it like a block: `mixerScale` / `setMixerScale`, view state), the master's Out; `Edge`s: notes, chain, route (graph `out → in`), send (`post / pre → s<id>`, a send at −∞ drawn dim), modulation (T4.7's wires; across tracks the graph's `m<id>`), each mapped to its graph wire (`graphWire`). **Re-layout cost** (PF.2): the build indexes the project's wires by track once (a group's passes read its track's wires), and the view's `ModelCache` (`LayoutOptions::layoutCache`) keeps for the next build the tables derived from the document alone (wires by track, each track's main path / stages / racks, a closed group's inner ids and crossing ports — reused while `LayoutOptions::snapshot` and `::lowering` are the same ones: a resize, nudge or fold) and each opened group's `graph::autoLayout` result keyed by the exact bytes of its inputs (sizes, port names, pin rows, the wires among its contents: any change inside it lays it out again; unused entries dropped); a closed group drawn as its panel makes no miniature. 64 tracks: an S-drag step ~6–9 ms (was ~600–800). Far zoom: a group panel under `GroupPanelFace::kStandInPx` (6 device px) draws its plate, its elements' static layers and per element one swatch of its live layer's mean colour (remade when its live key or the scale changes by a quarter). `checkAgainstGraph` proves the match (bands ↔ graph tracks and roles, blocks ↔ calls, every graph wire exactly one edge of its kind between the right nodes, groups contain their members). **Layout** (world units): bands stacked in lane order (members indented under their group), nodes on the band's spine left → right; a group's chain starts right of its members' mixers (its container surrounds them); returns right of every main track, the master right of everything; wires orthogonal, every wire into one Σ on one bus (one net: junction dots where feeds join); then `ViewState::patchNudges` (band by track id, block by device id). `buildProjectScene` turns it into a `circuit::CircuitScene` (model node = scene node, edge = scene wire; an expanded device contributes its patch's own `buildScene` inside its frame, read-only) drawn by `ProjectCanvas` (a `CircuitCanvas`: levels of detail, symbol tiles, Far chunks) with the bands / containers / frames in `paintUnderlay` and clip strips, mixer controls, bypass veils, z⁻¹ badges, mid-level titles, cost labels, drag previews and the wire readout in `paintOverlay`. **Edits** go through the same document commands as the Ableton view (one undo step each): export pin → device = `ops::connectWire` (a param menu), mixer `out` → Σ = `SetTrackOutput` (group / master) or a send to 0 dB (return), send pin → its return, band menu Add Device (`ops::addDevice`), Delete (`ops::deleteDevices`, wires removed, sends to −∞, routes to Sends Only: one Batch), block drag (`ops::moveDevice`, within or across bands), `0` (`ops::toggleDevicesActive`), M / S (`toggleTracksMute`, `soloTrack`), volume / pan / send drags (store gestures). **Semantic zoom**: the canvas's levels plus `focusBand` / `fitAll` (animated view changes) and devices opened to their atoms in place. **Morph** (`kMorphSeconds`, frame-driven): each lane's rect interpolates to its band's (the start view is chosen so the first visible lane's band lands where the lane was and bands are about lane height), clips collapse into their source's strip, nodes fade in; reversible mid-way. **Readouts / cost**: hovering a wire shows what the existing taps know (a route / send: the source track's `MeterSource` level; a modulation wire: amount and the target's `ModulationSource` sweep); per-device CPU labels through `DeviceCostSource` (`setShowCosts`, polled at 4 Hz while shown and live; the app's `ProjectLink` meters each unit schedule with one `engine::CostMeter` bound to every image of that schedule as it is published, freed 10 s after the schedule stops running, and splits readings per member track with T4.5's `attributeCost`; metering only while the labels are shown). **User devices** (T4.4-fix1): the view lowers with `LowerOptions::devices`; a user device's block is what the graph calls (its resolved root; title "(edited)", `Node::edited`), opened to its atoms it shows that patch, its param menu is the resolution's `infoOf`; the block menu has Edit Device Circuit (`editDeviceCircuit` = `circuit::openDeviceInCircuitView`: the project-device editor, edits are document commands, a stock device forks; Open in Circuit View is `openCircuit`, T5.x-one-circuit) and, for an edited device, Revert to Stock. Hooks: T4.8 racks (a rack is a device block until it opens to its chains). Scenes `project-patch-overview`, `project-patch-band` (`buildSketchProject`: the user's sketch). Theme: existing `circuit.*`, `mixer.*`, `arrangement.*` tokens. TMM.10-fix2 (SPEC §15 2026-11-01 "the note source is a Hz piano roll"): a MIDI track's note source (TL.1: its lane node, titled "Notes"; never MIDI; also the chain circuit's input terminal) has besides `notes` the note outputs `doc::kNoteOutputs` (Hz, gate, velocity: TL.1 the lane's port chips, `lanePortChip`), each a wire source exactly like an export: Crosshair on it, a drop on a param pin a wire drives connects that param (`Port::param`), a drop on a device its param menu (`openParamMenu(…, notes)`), `connectNotes` → `ops::connectNoteWire`, one undo step; the `notes` pin and a drop on a non-device are refused (NotAllowed, status). Its wires (Modulation edges from the window, `voice`) always take the target band's title strip; hovering names them ("Keys notes: Hz → …", the reducer). Scene `project-patch-notes`. **TX.patching**: a device's every output pin starts a wire (its main output too); a drop on any input pin (an audio input, a wired meta input, a param pin) wires that port, on a face's control (`CircuitCanvas::faceParamAt` over `MetaFace::paramRect`) that param, on a block elsewhere a menu of its params and "Input: …" entries; refused (NotAllowed, status) onto an instrument's `notes` pin or a rack's own input. Free devices (`Node::free`) sit on a row under their band's chain (`metrics::kFreeRowGap`; nudges move them anywhere), with no chain edge; the band menu's Add Free Device (`ProjectPatchView::addFreeDevice`: the add and the take-out in one Batch), the block menu's Take Out of Chain / Put Into Chain, a series block dragged below its chain's row leaves the series, a free block dragged in its band moves (a nudge) or, dropped on the chain's row, goes back in (`putIntoChain`: one step). `checkAgainstGraph` accepts a param's wires at `<param>_add`. An added wire's readout says "adds in the target's units" and its movement in units. Scene `project-patch-free`. **TX.panel-faces** (bug report 2026-11-01_12-15-15; QUESTIONS Q?-TX.panel-faces-a): a closed stock device (not edited, not a rack, not a chain meta device, not missing; designed `.panel.json` panels and generated ones alike) is drawn as its Device-view panel (`Node::panel`: the block is `devices::PanelFace::sizeFor(desc)` — the panel without its jack strip — and taller when its pins need the rows; `LayoutOptions::panelSize` from the view's template cache, `common/panel_template.h` shared with the Device view); opened to its atoms it shows the atoms. The scene lists it in `CircuitScene::faces` (`SceneFace::panel`, owner = device id, path = its patch's title; no pin stubs) and `CircuitCanvas::panelMaker` = `ProjectPatchView::makePanel` makes a `devices::PanelFace` over a `PatchPanelTarget` (the Device view's semantics: a param drag one store gesture, consts / activator one command each, a knob a macro drives turns its macro, face controls through `DocTarget`) — hosted by the one face path below; `syncPanels` (after every rebuild and param-only change; devices whose `DevicePtr` is unchanged skipped) pushes values, activation, name, free marker and the block's size. A wire dropped on a panel's control drives that param (`faceParamAt` → `Face::paramAt` → `DevicePanel::paramAt`); elsewhere on the block, the param menu as before. Blocks drawn as panels are label-free at Mid / Far (the panel has its title), and a switched-off device is not veiled (the panel shows its own off look). `setPanelsShown(false)` draws plain blocks (benchmarks' A/B). Not shown in panels yet: modulation rings, automated knob positions, the output spectrum (the target gives none). Scene `project-patch-panels`. **TX.mod-all** (the user: cables go to the knob they modulate, not to made-up ports): on a face or panel block a wired param whose face has a control for it has NO pin — `Port::anchored` with `(ax, ay)`, the control's centre (node-local) from `LayoutOptions::controlAnchor` (the view's `controlAnchorOf`: the shown face's `Face::controlHome`, a panel's control shown now, else a prototype face per meta type / panel per patch); anchored ports take no pin row; `inputEntry` / `entrySide` put the wire's end on the block's edge nearest the control (left, top or bottom, never the outputs' right edge) and `route` reaches it along the band's title strip (top) or under the chain row (bottom). The scene pin is invisible (its label stays for the face's "driven" look) and the scene wire names the param (`SceneWire::control`); `CircuitCanvas` draws the rest over the face at every level (`controlWires`, after the faces): from the edge to wherever `Face::controlAnchor` puts the control now (a slider's handle, an envelope's breakpoint, the panel's visible knob), cut at its rim with a plug dot, in the wire's colour and style (hover / selection follow it); a control not shown now (another tab or band, a setting hiding it) gets a socket dot on the edge. **TX.resize** (SPEC §15 2026-11-01 "resize, groups, and the free-device rack"): hold S over a closed device block (face, panel or plain block) and drag: `ProjectPatchView::setBlockScale` stores a uniform scale in the block's nudge entry (view state: saved, not undoable; `clampBlockScale`: 0.05 … 3 (TX.block-drag; 0.5 before), whole percent, a detent at 1, Shift steps of 25 %) and re-lays out (`relayout`: model + scene, no lowering). The model scales the block (`Node::scale`: `w`, `h`, `pinOffset` rows and `Port::ax / ay` anchors; `Builder::scaled`; an opened device keeps 1) and the layout makes room: the blocks after it move right, its band's spine moves down to the lowest scaled main pin (so a scaled block's top meets the title strip and chain wires stay straight) and the band grows; routes follow the scaled pins and anchors. The scene carries it (`circuit::SceneNode::scale`) and `CircuitCanvas` hosts a scaled face or panel at `scale ×` its px per unit in its own units (`local = (world − origin) / scale`: hit tests, pointer events, jacks, live rects, `controlAnchor` legs, `prepare` / `needsPrepare` / `staticAt`); its static-layer tiles are its own (`Hosted::tileSalt`) and a scale change settles like a zoom (stretched until `kZoomSettleSeconds`, then the tiles and a panel's layout at the new scale, so text and 1-px lines are crisp). The gesture: `ProjectCanvas::Drag::Resize` (`resizeHeld` = `UiTree::keyHeld('s')` and no text field focused; `resizableAt`), taken before a face's controls; the bottom-right corner follows the pointer along the press-time diagonal; Esc / focus loss restore the press's scale; S + double-click and the block menu's Reset Size give 1; Reset Layout / Reset Position keep sizes. Plain blocks scale their box and pin rows; their labels stay in the UI font (as at any zoom). Scene `project-patch-resize`. Pins stay for plain blocks (edited, missing or opened devices, racks' inner devices) and while faces are hidden. Hover, selection and Delete act on the wire's part outside the face; a drop onto a control wires it (`faceParamAt`). TX.cables: every edge between blocks is a cable (`Edge::cable` = `isCable(kind, from)`: not a route / send and not from a mixer): the model gives it its two ends (`points`: the source pin, the target pin or an anchored param's control centre), the scene its rest shape and key (`cableKeyOf`: kind, the ends' identities and port names), the canvas its motion; routes, sends and a mixer's output stay orthogonal traces. The view's `cableLevels`: a wire into a param glows with its modulation sweep (`ModulationSource`), an audio or note path (chain, notes, an input, terminals) with its track's peak level (`MeterSource`, −48 … 0 dB); `cableSignalLive` = meters or modulation live; `refreshTransport` / `wakeFaces` wake the glow. **TX.block-drag**: a block drag (MoveDevice; Ctrl: Nudge; Shift: Splice) moves the block itself live — `ProjectCanvas::liftDrag` lifts the dragged model nodes (`pickDragDevices`: the pressed block, or every selected block when it is selected; an opened capsule with its contents; a band by its title with all its nodes) through `CircuitCanvas::setNodeDrag`; its panel / face, frames, lane, mixer strip, labels and cables are drawn at the pointer each frame, nothing re-laid out; the drop is one nudge (a selection: one re-layout, `nudge(span)`; onto another band `moveToTrack(devices, track, at)`: one undo step); Esc / capture loss put it back. TX.drag-grow: while it moves, the band and the opened capsules the drop will put it in grow (or shrink back) live by the layout's own rule from the dragged rects (`Node::frameX0…` / `Band::baseH`, `baseX1`, `mixerX0`: the extents before growing; `ProjectCanvas::updateGrow`, O(nodes) per move, no re-layout): capsule frames, their In / Out terminals and an unplaced mixer follow, the bands under a grown band shift (`setNodeShift`), group containers follow; the drop lays out what was shown (in place / into or out of a capsule exactly; onto another band only that band grows); push-apart is not previewed. **Blocks never overlap** (`ProjectPatchView::startPush`, the node menu's push-apart made the one rule): every rebuild (a move, drop, spawn, resize, nudge or layout change; not while a block is dragged or resized — their release settles, `settleOverlaps`) collects each level's bodies (a band's blocks outside capsules with its source, mixer and Out standing and its title strip a wall — none in a group panel or the free rack; each opened capsule's members with its terminals standing), finds overlapping ones by a sweep (`kPushDetect` 0: touching is not overlapping), solves the separation to `kPushGap` (each pair along its axis of least overlap, shared by mass: area, a just-moved block ¼, a just-resized one ×4; a wall-blocked pair moves the lower one down; after 64 rounds everything resolves downwards, which ends) and glides there (critically damped, one re-layout per frame); a capsule or band the push grew into others gets the next round (≤ `kPushCascade`); a push no gesture caused (a project opened overlapping) does not mark the project changed. S-resize spans `doc::kMinPatchScale` 0.05 … 3. A device block smaller than `ProjectCanvas::kMinHitPx` across is hit at that size, and its pins reach `kSmallPinReach` of its size (its middle is the block). z⁻¹ badges ride the cable halfway along it. Scenes `project-cables`, `project-cables-far`, `project-cables-glow` (`--project tests/views/fixtures/meta-cables`: the user's meta patch). **Track lanes** (TL.1, SPEC §15 2026-11-08 "track lanes in the circuit view", docs/plans/TL.md): every band ENDS IN ITS TRACK'S LANE inside it (`Band::laneY` / `laneH`; default `metrics::kLaneH`, the height dragged at its bottom edge kept as view state `doc::PatchNudge::laneH`, file key "laneH"), every band's over ONE x range (`ProjectPatch::laneX0 … laneX1`: right of the deepest indent to the widest band's end; every band reaches it); the Notes / Audio source node and its lane window are gone — a MIDI / audio track's source is the lane itself (`NodeKind::Lane`, `band.source`, hidden scene node: no block, no pins, not hit; edges keep their `from`). Group / return / master lanes have no clip row (only automation sub-lanes). **Automation sub-lanes (TL.2)**: under a band's lane, one per parameter the ARRANGEMENT shows under the track (`ArrangementAutomation`'s per-track shown lanes, `lanesFor`, and their heights `laneHeight`, logical px = world units: one state for both views; a return / the master too, which the arrangement's pinned rows never show), in the order shown, as wide as the lane (`Band::subH` their total, `LayoutOptions::subLanes` per track id; `laneBlock(b)` holds lane + sub-lanes; a folded band keeps them; `ProjectPatchView::subLanes()` = band, param, index, world y / h; `subLaneRect(track, param)` and `controlDevice(binding)` give TL.3's wire its ends). **Automation wire (TL.3)**: `ProjectPatchView::automationWire()` (device px: `from` on the control's rim, `to` the nearest point of its sub-lane clamped to the canvas's visible x range, `normal`, `lane`) for the source `autoWire_` (track, param, control; unbound control = the track's mixer: `mixerControlAnchor`) set by `showParamAutomation(track, p, control)` (a band press on a face / panel control, value block, mixer volume / pan / send); `automationWirePress` (every left press in the circuit outside the lanes) ends it unless the press is on that control or on its block (a drag), `clearAutomationWire` on Esc. Followed as drawn now: the node's lift / shift offset (`nodeOffset`), the band's `bandNow` / `bandLifted`, a folded band's title strip; a control inside a closed group anchors at the group panel's element showing it, else at the group block's edge. `paintAutomationWire` in the canvas overlay: the cable look's rest shape (`cableRest`, table) at 0.75 sag eased to straight past ~60 deg, Catmull-Rom, fixed-size buffers; additive bloom, hairline core in `automation.line` with a hot centre, a ring round the control, a landing tick; its own damage box (a fader cap moving repaints only its face). Draw-only. Re-laid out (model + scene, the lowering kept) when the shown set or a height changes (`subLanesChanged`, from the lane surface's `damageAll` = the arrangement's `rowsChanged`; on show). The band's title strip has an automation toggle after the fold triangle (`ProjectCanvas::subLaneButtonRect`: `showLanes`), the band menu Show / Hide Automation Lanes and Add Automation Lane; a sub-lane's bottom edge resizes it (`LaneEdgeHit::Kind::SubBottom`, `setLaneHeight`); each is drawn in the arrangement's lane colour with the bar lines, a separator and its parameter's name (`paramName`). **A press on a control shows its sub-lane** (plan 9): a left press on a face's or panel's control or a one-control block (`controlAt`; not a param pin, which starts a cable) calls `showControlAutomation` → `ViewContext::showAutomation(track, ParamRef::device(...))` (a native engine's inner `meta` param has none; a macro-mapped param only with a lane of its own); the mixer face's volume / pan / send presses `showParamAutomation` with the track param. The arrangement takes the request as before (`showParam`: the lane added to the track's shown lanes, chosen for A mode), so the band shows it. A FOLDED band (`PatchNudge::folded`, "folded"; the title strip's triangle, the band menu's Fold / Fold All) is its title strip and its lane: its nodes `Node::folded` (hidden scene nodes, not hit, not pushed), the edges touching them no points. **One time window**: `ViewContext::timeWindow` (start, span ticks; `setTimeWindow` clamps, notifies `view_change::kTimeWindow`) is shared with the arrangement, which maps it onto its lanes' width (`pxPerTick = width / span`; shown, a new width keeps its zoom and the span follows; hidden it follows the span); the lanes map it onto `laneX0 … laneX1` on the canvas. A zoom / scroll repaints the lanes and the ruler (no re-layout). **Ruler**: the arrangement's `arrangement::Ruler` over an `arrangement::RulerAxis` (the arrangement's lanes, or `LaneRulerAxis`: lane_edit.cpp), a child of the canvas along its top (it draws and takes presses only over the lanes' x range; fits leave it free: `rulerInset`). **Lane cables**: a source wire's cable leaves the lane's TOP EDGE at `Edge::tapX` — the wire's `doc::Wire::tap` (band-local world x, file key "tap", `doc::SetWireTap`: Meta, never lowered; the view keeps its lowering: `tapOnly_`), else under the block it feeds (below where it plugs in, several spread 12 apart, right of the port chips). `ProjectCanvas::laneEdgeAt`: the lane's bottom edge (resize, `Drag::LaneSize`), a cable's end (`Drag::Tap`: slid along the edge in one store gesture = one undo step; double-click: back to the default), a MIDI lane's port chips (notes, Hz, gate, vel over the edge's left end: a cable of that output), the rest of the top edge (a notes / audio cable from there): the new wire is stamped with that tap (`pendingTap_` → `stampTap` in `connectEnds`, `SpawnLink::tap`). Over a lane Ctrl+wheel zooms time, Shift+wheel scrolls it; a face over a lane (a popup) keeps the pointer (`faceTakes`). **Automated controls** (TL.6): every control whose param has a lane (a meta face's, a closed group's / embedded panel's, a value block's, the mixer face's volume / pan / sends) shows the envelope at the playhead (`refreshAutomation` after a rebuild, a document or override change, `rebindFaces`, being shown; `applyAutomation` from the shared `AutomationFollow`'s 30 Hz poll while playing, and on `view_change::kTransport`); each tick damages only what moved (a panel's `GroupPanelFace::changedRects`, the mixer control's rect, a meta face whole: `CircuitCanvas::damageFace`).
- **Meta-module faces** (TMM.4, docs/plans/MM.md "UI"; `pq/ui/face_painter.h`, `pq/views/meta/meta_face.h`, `face_targets.h`, `src/views/src/meta/`). A face is vector code in its own world units, drawn through `ui::FacePainter` (world → device over the draw list and `lines.h`: discs, rings, arcs, polylines, dashes, fills under curves, convex polygons, additive glow; hinted text at round(px · scale) with a bar below 4 device px; the same calls at every scale, `primitives()`), its ≤ 3 value-independent STATIC layers rasterised per exact zoom into coverage tiles (`recordLayer` / `drawLayer`, shared by every face of a type, stretched while a zoom settles) and its LIVE layer drawn as geometry each frame. `views::MetaFace` (one class per type, registered by type id: `makeFace`, `hasFace`, `faceSize`, `registerFace`): `worldSize`, `inputY` / `outputY` (pin rows), `paintStatic` / `paintLive` / `paintJacks` (the host's pins as sockets: one look), `liveRects`, `hitTest` (a control part, `kFrame`, `kNone`), `onPointer` (relative drags in device px with a 150-logical-px floor per full travel, so a control drags alike at zoom 0.05 and 3; Shift fine; double-click resets), `cursor`, `describe` (the info view); bound to a `MetaTarget` (`DocTarget`: an instance (device, name path; "" = a chain meta device), values through `doc::metaParamValue`, a drag = one store gesture of `ops::setMetaParam`, never a compile; `DefaultTarget`: the type's defaults, read-only) and a `VoiceTapReader` (the narrow views-side interface TMM.2's taps feed: per voice slot the newest sampled phase plus the phases sampled since the last read, `serial`, `live`; `SyntheticVoices` for scenes, tests and benchmarks; TMM.5 `TapVoices` (`pq/views/meta/tap_voices.h`) adapts `pq::VoiceTapReader`: the tap re-resolved through a callback at each poll (a recompile may replace it; a tap that appears later is followed), `serial` = the records written (relaxed loads) and a bump when the shown voices go stale, `live` = a voice at the last read or records since, `fresh` for the host's wake check; TMM.16: `VoiceTapReader::heardTable()` → `HeardTable{table, generation, modulated}`, the table the instance's slot plays now — `TapVoices` gets it through a second resolver (`app::FaceVoiceHub`: `ProjectLink::metaFrameTable(device, path, 0, modulated)`), a new publish counts as news (`serial` changes, `fresh` until read, `live` for `kTableLinger` = 2 s so the full table after a preview is drawn too), and `FaceVoice::increment` carries the read's increment (its mip level)). The Wavetable face draws what is heard (TMM.16, `wavetable_model.h` `wt::Heard`): with a `HeardTable`, its highlighted frame, the voices' dots, draw mode's cycle and the stacked thumbnails are that table read through the readers' interpolator at the level the lowest sounding voice reads (level 0 while nothing sounds), the live path's controls (`meta::wavetableLiveControls`) applied on top, Lanczos-σ smoothed to the screen's points (`wt::smoothFrame`); without one (no engine) its own `wt::Model` renders them from the state the same way (full-resolution sounding frames, lazily). **Hosting**: `circuit::describe` sizes a call of a meta type with a face by the face (`SymbolSpec::face`, pins at the face's rows, no stubs), `buildScene` lists it in `CircuitScene::faces` (`SceneFace{node, type, path, owner}`; label-free: only the binding name stays above it); the project patch model draws a chain meta device (`Node::face`, `Node::meta`) at the face's size and `buildProjectScene` lists it (owner = device id, path "") plus the meta calls inside opened devices. TX.panel-faces: what the canvas hosts is a `views::Face` (the host-facing interface split out of `MetaFace`: geometry, static layers (+ `staticAt(scale)`), live layer, jacks, hit test, pointer, keys, text (`onText` / `wantsText`, mirrored by the canvas's `acceptsTextInput`), `focusLost`, cursor, info, `paramAt` (wire drops), `controlAnchor(param)` / `controlHome(param)` (TX.mod-all: where a wire into the param ends — the control's centre and rim radius now, and where it is placed whatever the face's state; `MetaFace` from `paramRect`, the Wavetable's knobs / handles exactly, `devices::PanelFace` from `DevicePanel::controlAnchor` (a `PanelKnob`'s arc) / `controlHome` (the description's control for the param under any selector value, bound by name first)), and the face's own clock: `attach(FaceHost*)`, `update(now)`, `nextWake()`, `needsPrepare` / `prepare(scale)` once a zoom settles); `MetaFace` derives from it and device panels (`devices::PanelFace`: the panel's own tree laid out at the canvas scale ≥ 0.5, recorded once per change and replayed with `appendTransformed`, below 0.5 four colour-class coverage tiles through the static-layer path) are hosted by the same code. `faceCount` / `faceAt` / `faceOn` list the meta faces, `panelCount` / `panelAt` / `panelOn` the panels; `Stats::panelsPainted`. `CircuitCanvas` hosts every scene face (`faceBinder` → `FaceBinding{target, voices}`, `rebindFaces`, `faceOn`, `wakeFaces`, `setFacesShown` for A/B benchmarks): faces are left out of the Far chunks and drawn over them at every level (zooming out removes nothing), their static layers from tiles (built with the symbol tiles in `onFrame`), jacks and live layer as geometry; `facePointer` (called first by `CircuitCanvas`, `ProjectCanvas` and `EditCanvas::onPointer`) routes presses on controls, drags (Esc / capture loss cancel them), hover and wheels a face uses; over the frame the node behaves as any block (select, move, double-click: open to its atoms; a chain meta device's atoms are its generated expansion, read-only, its title strip closes it). While a visible face's reader is `live`, a ≤ 60 Hz timer polls the readers (`MetaFace::pollVoices`, never from paint) and damages only the faces' `liveRects`; otherwise nothing runs. The circuit editor parses with the meta prelude's signatures (typing `wt1 = meta_wavetable()` works, the check's library holds the prelude patches) (GU.f7: its faces show the types' defaults; `CircuitEditor::Host` is gone). (GU.4: the Phase accumulator face is deleted with its type: the Phase Accumulator module's face is its group panel of four generic knobs, `pq/document/modules.h`; scene `project-meta-faces`.) **TMM.7 additions**: `MetaTarget` also reads and edits settings (`setting`, `setSetting`: one step, structural) and state data (`data`: never null for a type with data, the same pointer while unchanged, so faces cache by it; `setData` inside begin / end joins the gesture), says why a gesture is refused (`notice` → the status line) and names its type (`targetType`); `FaceVoice::position` carries the position the writer reported (the Wavetable's frame). `MetaFace::staticKey(layer)` gives a per-instance static layer (0 = shared by the type): the canvas keys its tile by (type, layer, key), prunes stale keys, and draws such a layer directly while its face drags; `MetaFace::onKey` gets keys while the face holds the canvas's key focus (the last press went to it; `CircuitCanvas::faceKey`, called first by `CircuitCanvas::onKey` and `ProjectCanvas::onKey`); `voicesPolled` lets a face animate on its own (fades) and `setClock` makes that testable; `paintFace` paints a face without a host. A new scene **keeps the faces** of the instances it still shows (same type, owner, path) with their bindings, state and drag (a face's own setting edit rebuilds the host synchronously); faces that go are retired until the next paint, so a handler returns into a live object. The **Wavetable face** (`wavetable_face.cpp`, 204 × 248; its display model `wavetable_model.h`: keyframe spectra cached by cycle hash, dense frames by the builder's rules at display resolution with σ-smoothing, the bake through `dsp/wave_warp.h`, the frame blend, keyframe harmonics, the source cycle at a position): mode switch and Basic Shapes chooser, read-only harmonic rows (TMM.9 edits), the stacked dense frames (static layer 3, keyed by data + bake) with the frame at the position highlighted, per voice a dot / dotted bar / glow density on its own frame (80 ms fades), the position slider with keyframe markers and gap glyphs, the phase slider (per-voice ticks when wired), fold / warp / sync knobs and the warp-mode switch. Scene `project-meta-wavetable`; goldens `meta-wavetable-z<zoom>-<scale>x`, `meta-wavetable-poly-z2-<scale>x`, `meta-wavetable-voices` (engine taps, `tests/app/test_meta_wavetable.cpp`). **TMM.9 additions**: `FacePointer::button` — the canvas routes right presses to a face, which takes them only where it uses them (else the host's menu opens), and hover moves without a drag reach `onPointer(Move)` (a face tracks its own hover; true = repaint). The Wavetable face's **harmonic editor** (two rows of bars for the edited keyframe — amplitude, linear with Alt showing dB, and phase about a sine —, 32 bars at first, wheel scrolls / Ctrl+wheel zooms on a √2 ladder up to all 1024 with DC, a scrollbar; drag paints, Shift draws a line, right-click / right-drag / double-click resets; bars thinner than 1.6 device px merge into columns of the loudest) and **draw tools** (pencil, line, Catmull-Rom curve, snap grid off / 8 / 16 / 32, the table's normalise and remove-DC flags) work on one keyframe's 2048 samples through `src/views/src/meta/wavetable_edit.h` (a harmonic edit = the old samples + a double iFFT of the edited harmonics' change: others change only by float rounding; strokes re-render from the stroke's start). The edited keyframe is the selected one, or after a position drag the one at the position (between keyframes, the first edit makes one from the blended frame: one undo step). Seven static layers (the harmonic slots, amplitude bars and phase bars per instance, keyed by data and view; in draw mode layer 3 is the grid). Goldens `meta-wavetable-harmonics-<scale>x`, `meta-wavetable-draw-<scale>x`. The **Envelope face** (TMM.11, `envelope_face.cpp`, 208 × 128): the shape drawn from the DSP's continuous-time law (attack, decay, a fixed-width sustain plateau, release; time log-scaled per segment by each time's control travel, the three segments fitted to the display ×1 … ×4 — the fit frozen while a drag runs), three breakpoints (peak: attack; decay's end: decay ↔ and sustain ↕ in one gesture; release's end: release), the plateau (sustain), a bend ring per segment (and the segment's line itself: its curve), the amount slider; hit-testing picks the nearest target (so every one is reachable at zoom 0.1); per voice a glowing dot on the shape at its level and stage (rising = attack, from the tap's sampled levels; released = release), a drop line and the last frame's sampled levels as a trail (80 ms fades). Scenes `device-meta-envelope`, `project-meta-envelope`; goldens `meta-envelope-z<zoom>-<scale>x`. (GU.4: the VCA face is deleted with its type: the VCA module's face is its group panel — a 360° knob with the readout inside and a curve editor on its value block's curve; goldens `module-vca-panel`, `module-phase-acc-panel`, scene `project-vca-panel`.)
- **MM review UI pass** (TMM.13-fix2, docs/reviews/MM.md H1, H5, H6, M5, M9, M10, L14, L21–L23, "UI and aesthetic"; QUESTIONS Q?-TMM.13-fix2-a). *Lifetimes*: `DeviceChainView::sync` retires replaced frames (`Impl::retiredFrames`) and drops them in `onFrame`, never inside the store listener (a group panel's own key handler dispatched the edit that removed it); `ProjectPatchView::dispatch` reads nothing of `this` after the store call; an embedded `ProjectCanvas` (group panel, free rack) reports Delete / Ctrl+G / Ctrl+Shift+G / 0 handled only when they acted, and a group panel never offers its own group to Ctrl+G. *Wires*: one palette in both views — theme `circuit.wireAudio` (the audio path), `circuit.wireMod` (modulation into a param, mint), `circuit.wireNotes` (the note source's, amber; `circuit::WireStyle::notes`); the project patch model routes parallel runs 6 units apart, and a wire into a face / panel control (`ControlAnchor::clear`, `LayoutOptions::Anchor`, `Port::aclear`) runs down the gap beside the control (its labels' half width + 3) and enters it from the left (`entrySide`: the left edge only for a control in the face's first column). The Device view's cables take the same colours by role (`CableLayer::Cable::role`), a wire's cable is drawn at 35 % unless its badge is hovered or it is selected, one with an end in the collapsed free rack is drawn only then (`tucked`: its badge sits beside the chain end's control), and each chain link also draws the audio path through the device before it (`through`, a 1.5 px line along the frames' feet). *Faces*: `Face::captionAt` (where a host's caption — a circuit binding name, a scoped canvas's device name — sits, face-local; `faceCaptionAt(type)`), `Face::jackInset` (the analysers' sockets on the frame's edge), `Face::ownsPlate` (a Device-view panel draws the family's rounded body behind a face without one); theme `face.ink` (unselected glyphs and marks: the Wavetable's glyph layer 7); `DevicePanel::kFaceMaxScale` 2. *Canvas*: `CircuitCanvas::setFitAlignLeft` (the free rack), terminal pills hidden while every face is drawn under three pill rows tall, band names as pills over the blocks when their strip is too thin, the lane window title in its own row (bar numbers start right of it). *Cost*: `DocTarget` finds its device once per document snapshot; `FacePainter::dashed` draws one stroke at the pattern's mean coverage below 2 device px a period; the Wavetable face memoises its sounding frame by (position, displayed key).
- **Face panels** (TMM.10; GU.11 `panel_layout.h` `facePanel`, `src/views/src/devices/face_display.cpp`, `DevicePanel::logicalWidthFor` / `controlRect`, `DeviceTarget::metaType / metaTarget / metaVoices`): a native engine on a track's path shows its face as its Device-view frame's body, scaled to the frame's height; the face's controls answer `controlRect` / `paramAt` (wires end on them).
- **Chain circuit** (T5.x-circuit-chain, `pq/views/circuit/chain_circuit.h`, `chain_paint.h`; `src/circuit/chain_circuit.cpp`, `chain_paint.cpp`; QUESTIONS Q164, Q165). **Model** (`buildTrackCircuit(project, track, {DeviceLibrary, ChainSceneCache})` / `buildRackCircuit(project, rack, …)` → `ChainCircuit`, plain data, UI thread): a `ChainBox` per device (Device: its patch's own `buildScene` inside, from a `ChainSceneCache` keyed by the patch played — a user device's resolved root —, so a rebuild after a structure change composes cached scenes; Rack: its `Chain` boxes stacked, each holding its devices; any depth, laid out iteratively), `ChainLink`s (Audio: a device's `out` terminal → the next device's first audio `in`; Notes; a rack's split bus into every chain and summing bus into its Σ; Modulation: T4.7 wires in a gutter above the boxes), terminals, `boxOfNode`, hit tests (`boxAt`, `deviceBoxAt`). The composed scene copies each part's junctions (`indexScene(…, junctionNetsFrom)`). The project patch view draws `buildRackCircuit`'s circuit inside an opened rack's block (`paintChainBoxes` / `paintChainVeils`). T5.x-one-circuit removed T5.x-circuit-chain's separate Track-level view (`TrackCircuitView`, `ChainCanvas`, `openTrackCircuit`, `openDeviceChainCircuit`, scene `circuit-track-chain`): the track level is now an opened band of the project patch view; `buildTrackCircuit` stays as the model of a whole chain (tests, bench).
- **One circuit view** (T5.x-one-circuit, SPEC §15 2026-10-31). `ViewContext::openProjectCircuit(track, device)` → the `CircuitOpener` `installProjectPatch` sets → `ProjectPatchView::openCircuit`: shown (at once, no morph, when hidden), the band opened (`expandBand`: every device to its atoms), framed on the device (`deviceWorldRect`: its frame, inside an opened rack its box) and focused (`focusedDevice`, outlined in `paintOverlay`); `closeBand` / `fitAll` clear the focus. Callers: the Device view (`DeviceChainView::openInCircuitView`: menu, title double-click), the project view's block menu, `view.trackCircuit` (Ctrl+Alt+C, now a `kPatchActions` id), the device editor's Track button (`device_circuit.cpp`). In the view a double-click inside an opened device edits its circuit (`editDeviceCircuit`; in an opened rack the device under the pointer), on its title strip closes it; modulation wires between opened devices of a band take the band's title strip (the gutter). **Lane windows** (superseded by TL.1, see "Project patch view" → Track lanes: each band ends in its lane over the shared time window; `paintLanes`, `scrollLanes` / `zoomLanes` on `ViewContext::timeWindow`, no re-layout on a zoom). Input: double-click a clip → `openClip` (detail view in Clip mode on it, the arrangement's selection, then `onOpenClip`: installProjectPatch focuses the piano roll or the audio Clip View). Playhead: `refreshTransport` on `view_change::kTransport` and show / hide; while playing a timer at about one device px of the windows (1/144 … 0.1 s) damages the old and new column of each visible lane (`damageLaneColumn`) and pages the windows on when the playhead runs out of them; stopped or hidden: no reads, no timers. Scenes `project-circuit-device`, `project-patch-lanes` (`--project tests/views/fixtures/audio`).
- **Lane editing** (TX.lane-edit, bug report 2026-11-01_13-50-52; TL.1: the band lanes; `src/views/src/arrangement/edit_surface.h`, `src/project_patch/lane_edit.cpp`; QUESTIONS Q?-TX.lane-edit-a). A band's lane is an editing surface of its track run by the ARRANGEMENT's own `arrangement::Editor` and `ArrangementAutomation` — no second implementation: one selection, one clipboard, one undo history for both views. The shared part is extracted as `arrangement::EditSurface` (what the editing code draws and hit-tests on: `widget()` for focus / menus / drag-out, `active()`, clip `rows()` in order with `rowAt` / `clampRow` / `rowLaneRect`, `viewport()`, a linear `TimeAxis` PER ROW (`x = x0 + (tick − t0)·k`), `gridPxPerTick`, `clipTitleHeight(row)`, `tiles()`, the arrangement's extras — `revealTick` / `revealRow`, lanes, pinned rows — empty elsewhere, `damageAll`, `menuItems` appended to the edit menu). `ArrangementView` owns `arrangement::Surfaces`: its own `ArrangementSurface` (the lanes; one axis for every row) plus registered ones, and the surface a call binds (`Surfaces::Bind`, the arrangement's when none). Editor and automation take every geometry from `geo()` = the running gesture's surface (`Drag::surface`, set at the press) else the bound one; selection and hover damage go to every ACTIVE surface on its own rows and axes (`Editor::damageRows` / `damageClip`, `ArrangementAutomation::damageTicks`: tick ranges, not px); `AutomationBand::row` carries the band's row so painting uses its surface's axis (`paintSurface`; envelope / preview tiles only on the arrangement's surface, `LanePaint::arrangementLanes`). The project patch view's `LaneEditSurface` (main view only; registered on show and on first use; unregistered by `~EditSurface`, and the arrangement's `Surfaces` unregister it when they go first): rows = the bands with a clip row (MIDI / audio) in band order (`bandTrack`, `bandTuning`; rebuilt per document version / model rebuild), each row's body its lane (`laneBodyDevice`; its `rowLaneRect` one px taller each side, as the arrangement's rows), one axis for all: the lanes' x range over the shared time window (`laneStart`, `laneSpan`), title strip `laneClipTitleH`; `revealTick` pages the shared window (the tick a quarter in). TL.1: a click on EMPTY lane space (any surface: `Editor::press`) places the insert marker and, stopped, the start marker and the playhead there (`setStartMarker`, `transport().locate`: Space plays from the clicked tick); while playing the insert marker only. TL.4: `Editor::placeStart(t)` — every plain left click on a lane's time (empty space, a clip's body, or a clip's title / edge released without a move: `Drag::clickTick`; not a clip drag, not Shift / Ctrl) sets the start marker there (snapped), stopped also the playhead; playing, playback goes on and the next Play starts there (TL.1 had missed clicks on clips: a click into a MIDI clip left Play at the old marker). The Play button (`ViewContext::play`) plays from the start marker while playing too (restarts there). Input (`ProjectCanvas::lanePointer`, after `laneEdgePointer`, before faces and blocks): a left / right press in a lane binds the surface and goes to the automation, then the editor (as `LaneCanvas::onPointer` does); a drag holds `Drag::Lane` until it ends; Esc / capture loss → `laneAbort` (no edit); a double-click on a clip still opens its editor; hover → `laneHover` / `laneCursor`. Keys: after a press in a lane (`laneFocused`) the chord is looked up in the `arrangement` keymap scope and run with the surface bound when it is one of the edit actions (`kLaneActions`: copy … deleteTime, toggleActive, selectAll, arrows, Esc, A; TL.1 the zoom chords zoomToSelection / zoomBack / zoomIn / zoomOut, on the shared window) and enabled; otherwise the canvas's own keys. Painting (`paintLanes`, from `paintUnderlay` under the blocks and cables; a band's lane where its strip is drawn now: `bandNow`): TL.4 a lane is `laneStrip` = its compact scrub ruler on top (`laneRuler`, `metrics::kLaneRulerH`, under the cable tap edge) and its clip row (`laneBody`, what the editor edits; `kLaneH` holds both, `kLaneMinH`); the body and sub-lanes get the arrangement's own background (`arrangement::paintGrid` at the lane's zoom: alternate-bar shading, dashed grid; selected tracks lighter), the lane and its sub-lanes one dark inset frame, a separator under the ruler, the track's colour bar left of it (lanes under 6 px: fill only; rulers under 5 px: a plain strip). The ruler strip is the ONE compact `arrangement::Ruler` (`setCompact`: numbers, ticks, start marker, playhead; no loop brace / marker row), a hidden canvas child (`ProjectPatchView::laneRuler`) painted into each lane (`Ruler::paintStrip`) and handed a press there and its gesture (`ProjectCanvas::laneRulerPointer`, `Drag::LaneRuler`, `Ruler::pointerStrip`): a click locates (snapped), a drag zooms / scrolls the shared window, as the top ruler; with the arrangement next to the view the clips are drawn by the arrangement's painters (`paintClip`, `paintAudioClip`, `paintAudioCrossfade`, `flushTiles` with `LanePaint::arrangementLanes = false`: no TileAtlas tiles, never "zooming"), with the selected tracks' lighter body, the time selection, a lane drag's previews (`Editor::dragSurface`), in automation mode `ArrangementAutomation::paintSurface` and the chosen parameter's name at the lane's top right; the playheads last. The arrangement's own lanes draw drag previews only for drags on their surface. TL.2: the surface's LANES are the bands' automation sub-lanes (TL.5: a follow lane re-pointed to a parameter of the same height re-points `ProjectPatchView::subLanes()` in place, `subLaneGeneration()` bumped, no re-layout; each sub-lane's label starts with its pin, `subLanePinDevice` / `toggleSubLanePin`; `LaneEditSurface::laneRows()` / `laneRect()`: device px, sorted top to bottom, `y` the window y = `laneRect(i).y`, `row` the band's clip row or −1 for a group / return / master; cached per model build, canvas view / rect, document snapshot and drag shift; a band dragged by its title has none meanwhile; `axis()` is the lanes' one x range, rows or not), so `ArrangementAutomation` hit-tests, edits, damages and paints them like its own lanes (`paintSurface` draws a surface's lanes always, its rows in A mode); `openMenu` reads the band's lane index on the band's surface and offers Hide This Lane there. A press in a sub-lane goes the lane's way (`laneEditAt`); `laneClipAt` reads only the clip row. Scenes `project-patch-lane-edit` (TL.2: Pad's volume sub-lane), `project-patch-lanes`.
